def lse_score_grad_raw(a,beta):
   n=len(a); c=fast_conv(a)
   S=a.sum(); if S small ...
   cmax=c.max()
   z=beta*(c-cmax); z-=z.max(); e=np.exp(z); p=e/e.sum(); L=cmax + np.log(e.sum())/beta
   score = 2*n*L/(S*S)
   # gradient of L wrt a:

   return score, ga
```

```
for iter:
  c=conv(a); kmax=argmax
  # choose set of peaks with c >= (1 - eps)*max (e.g., top 5)
  g = average of subgradients for selected peaks: for each k, h_i = a_{k-i}+a_{i-k} terms within valid (ordered pairs count 2 except diagonal). Compute via convolution of indicator with rev_a: h=2*conv(rev_a, indicators) at slice. If k diagonal care? convolution formula 2 works double counts? For max subgradient wrt C(max), subgradient of c_k is derivative at a: same 2 conv. If multiple peaks, average.
  Normalize a, project gradient to sum0, step small, project simplex, accept if exact score improves.
```

def run_lse_raw(a0,beta,max_iter=max_iter,max_fun=max_fun):
   a=np.clip(a0,0,1000)
   if a.sum()<1e-9: a=np.ones(n)/n
   bounds=[(0.0,1000.0)]*n  # but creating large list each call expensive; use [(0,None)]*n?
   options={'maxiter':max_iter,'ftol':1e-15,'gtol':1e-12,'maxfun':max_fun,'maxls':20}
   try:
      res=minimize(score_jac_binary, a, args=(beta,), method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, bounds=bounds, callback=callback_time, options=options)
      a=res.x
   except TimeLimit:
      a=_LAST_X
   a=np.clip(a,0,1000)
   S=a.sum()
   if S>0: a=a/S # normalization optional
   return a
```

Implement `simplex_project(v, z=1.0)`:
```
v=np.asarray(v)
mu=z
w=srt(v)[::-1]
sv=np.cumsum(w)
r=np.arange(1,len+1)
cond = w > mu - (sv-mu)/r
rho = cond.nonzero()[0][-1]
theta = (sv[rho] - mu)/(rho+1)
return np.maximum(v - theta, 0)
```
Use floating. This is exact for L1 simplex. O(n log n) each iter. Could be heavy if 5000 iters. But we can reduce peak refinement iters or use simple scale. Let's use simple scale for speed; it's common.

`simple_normalize_project(v, sum_target=1.0)`:
```
v=np.maximum(v,0)
s=v.sum()
if s > 0: v*=sum_target/s
else: v=sum_target/len
```
This may change relative values when many zeros but okay.

def fast_conv(a):
    n=a.shape[0]
    if n==1: return np.array([a[0]*a[0]])
    m = max(1,2*n-1)
    size = 1 << (m-1).bit_length()? Actually for convolution length 2n-1, need FFT size at least 2n-1. Use `1 << (2*n - 2).bit_length()` works if 2n-2>=1.     A=np.fft.rfft(a,size)
    return np.fft.irfft(A*A,size)[:2*n-1]
```

```
def conv_deriv(a,w):
    n=a.size
    size = 1 << (3*n - 3).bit_length()? Need length (n-1)+(len(w)-1)+1 = (n-1)+(2n-2)+1=3n-2. Use `1 << (3*n - 3).bit_length()`? For length L, need size >=L. If L=3n-2. `normalize(a, sum_target=1.0)`:
```
def normalize(a,sum_target=1.0):
   a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
   a=np.where(np.isfinite(a),a,0.0)
   a=np.clip(a,0.0,None)
   s=float(a.sum())
   if s < 1e-12:
       return np.full_like(a, sum_target/a.size if a.size else 0.0)
   return a*(sum_target/s)
```

`compute_score(a)`:
```
def compute_score(a):
   a=normalize(a)
   n=a.size
   if n==0: return inf
   c=fast_conv(a)
   return float(2.0*n*float(c.max()))
```

`lse_score_grad(a,beta)`:
Input not normalized perhaps. We'll compute S. If S<1e-12, return (1e100, zeros). But for gradient, if S small, use uniform? We'll guard.
```
def lse_score_grad(a,beta):
   a=np.asarray(a,dtype=float)
   n=a.size
   s=a.sum()
   if s <=1e-12:
       return 1e100, np.zeros(n)
   c=fast_conv(a)
   cm=float(c.max())
   # if beta finite? 
   z=beta*(c-cm)
   z-=z.max()
   e=np.exp(z)
   esum=e.sum()
   L=cm + np.log(esum)/beta
   score=2*n*L/(s*s)
   w=e/esum
   h=2.0*conv_deriv(a,w)
   ga=(2.0*n)*(h*s - 2.0*L)/(s**3)
   # clean nan
   if not np.all(np.isfinite(ga)): ga=np.zeros(n)
   return score,ga
```
But `beta` might be very large and c-cm small; z range maybe 0..-inf; exp okay. If beta=0? We won't.

`softmax_score_jac_v(x,beta)`:
```
def softmax_score_jac_v(x,beta):
   # x raw log; center, clip
   yr=x.astype(float)
   yr=yr-np.mean(yr)
   yr=np.clip(yr,-50.0,50.0)
   e=np.exp(yr)
   a=e/e.sum()
   sc,ga=lse_score_grad(a,beta)
   dot=float(np.dot(a,ga))
   gv=a*(ga - a*dot)
   if not finite: gv=zeros
   return float(sc),gv
```
Wait formula `a*(ga - a*dot)` where `a*dot` broadcasts. Correct.

`weighted_score_grad(a,w)`:
```
def weighted_score_grad(a,w):
   s=sum; if small...
   c=fast_conv(a)
   wn=float(np.dot(w,c))
   score=2*n*wn/s*s
   h=2*conv_deriv(a,w)
   ga=(2*n)*(h*s - 2*wn)/(s**3)
   return score,ga
```
Weighted objective can be less than 0? w,c>=0, score positive. But w normalized sum1 maybe not. fine.

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400):
  global _DEADLINE, _LAST_RAW_X, _LAST_V_X
  t0=time.monotonic(); total=float(budget_s); final_deadline=t0+total-60
  rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
  parent=None
  try: p=height_sequence_1 ...
  n=6962
  if parent is not None and parent.size>=100:
      # choose n = parent.size if 2000 <= size <= 12000? else 6962

      a=interpolate parent to n
  else: a=uniform? generate
  best_a=normalize(a); best_sc=compute_score(best_a)
  print
  # If no parent, or score poor, generate candidates
  if parent is None or best_sc > 1.6:
      candidates=[]
      # add best_a and generated
      for cand in generate_candidates(n,rng,parent):
           s=compute_score(cand)
           if s<current_best: update
      # Also run quick LSE on top 2? maybe skip to save.

  if best_sc > 1.5027:
      # generate 10 candidates, pick best 2 by score, run raw LSE beta=n*1 for 30s each
  # Main loop
  gamma_coeffs=[0.2,1.0,5.0,20.0,100.0]
  cycle=0
  main_end = final_deadline - 180  # reserve for peak subgrad + final
  last_best_time=t0
  while time.monotonic() < main_end:
      beta = int(n*gamma_coeffs[cycle%len])
      a_curr=best_a.copy()
      # perturbation if stale
      if time.monotonic()-last_best_time > 300:
           a_curr += rng.normal(0,0.02*max(1/n? no normalized scale?))
           a_curr=normalize(a_curr)
      # Choose method by cycle
      if cycle %3 ==0:
           a2=run_raw_lbfgs(a_curr,beta, min(main_end, time+120s), maxiter=1000)
      elif cycle%3==1:

           a2=run_weighted_lbfgs(a_curr,w,...)
      else:
           a2=run_v_lbfgs(a_curr,beta,...)
      update: sc=compute_score(a2); if sc<best_sc - 1e-13: best_a=a2; best_sc=sc; last_best_time=time
      # exact peak subgrad occasionally: if cycle%5==4, run 300 iters with deadline 60s
      cycle +=1
  # Peak subgradient refine
  if time < final_deadline-60:
      a2=peak_subgrad(best_a, max_iters=2000, time_limit=final_deadline-120)
  update
  # Final high-beta raw LSE short
  if time < final_deadline:
      beta=int(200*n)
      a2=run_raw_lbfgs(best_a,beta, final_deadline, maxiter=500)
  update
  # Verify final direct
  out=[float(x) for x in normalize(best_a)]
  try:
      ver=float(evaluate_sequence(out))
      print
      if ver != inf and ver < best_sc? update? best_sc=ver
  except: pass
  # fallback if evaluate bad?
  return out

def generate_candidates(n, rng, parent):
   cands=[]
   x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
   # parent interp already
   cands.append(np.ones(n))
   for a in [0.0,0.2,0.5,0.8]:
      f=1 + a*np.cos(2*np.pi*x)
      f=np.maximum(f,0.01); cands.append(f)
   for phase in [0, np.pi/2, np.pi, 3*np.pi/2]:
      f=1 + 0.5*np.cos(np.pi*x + phase)
      f=np.maximum(f,0.01); cands.append(f)
   for p in [0.5,1,2,4]:
      f=x**p; cands.append(f)
      f=(1-x)**p; cands.append(f)
      f=x**p*(1-x)**(0.5); cands.append(f)
   for r in [0.3,0.6,0.9]:
      f=np.maximum(r + (1-r)*x,0.01); cands.append(f)
   # random Fourier
   for _ in range(8):
      sig=0
      for m in [1,2,3,5,8,13]:
         amp=rng.uniform(0,0.3)/ (1+m*0.2)
         ph=rng.uniform(0,2π)
         sig += amp*np.cos(2π*m*x+ph)
      f=1+0.5*sig; f=np.maximum(f,0.01); cands.append(f)
   # parent perturb
   if parent is not None:
      for eps in [0.01,0.03,0.08]:
         f=normalize(parent_interp) + rng.normal(0,eps,n); f=np.maximum(f,0.01); cands.append(f)
   return cands
```
Normalize each before exact score. Many candidates ~40; exact score FFT each ~0.005s -> fine.

But some candidates uniform symmetric may not improve. We'll evaluate and keep top by score. Also ensure diversity? We can just pick best.

```
def peak_subgradient_refine(a0, max_iters=3000, time_limit=60, top_k=10):
   global _DEADLINE? Use local deadline param but cannot use closure; just check time.
   deadline = time.monotonic()+time_limit
   a=normalize(a0)
   best=a.copy(); best_sc=compute_score(a)
   v=np.zeros(n); step=0.01
   for it in range(max_iters):
      if time.monotonic()>deadline: break
      c=fast_conv(a); sc=2n*c.max(); if sc<best_sc update
      maxc=c.max()
      # active where >= maxc - 0.02 maxc (or top_k)
      thr=maxc - 0.02*maxc
      idx=np.where(c>=thr)[0]
      if idx.size==0: idx=[argmax]
      if idx.size>top_k: idx=np.argpartition(c, -top_k)[-top_k:]
      w=np.zeros(c.size); w[idx]=1/idx.size
      h=2*conv_deriv(a,w)
      g=h - h.mean()
      # momentum
      v=0.9*v + g
      gn=norm(v)
      if gn <1e-15: break
      v/=gn
      a_try=normalize(a - step*v)
      sc_try=compute_score(a_try)
      if sc_try < best_sc:
          best=a_try; best_sc=sc_try; step=min(step*1.05,0.1)
      else:
          step*=0.8
   return best
```
Note normalized sum1; `step` scale relative to gradient norm1. For maximum reduction, maybe step 0.01-0.1. This uses exact score each iteration (FFT). Good.

But if active region large, w average reduces plateau; may not lower max. Could use only top 1 or top 3. We'll vary `top_k` and drain in calls:
- Call with top_k=1 drain 0 to reduce global max.
- Call with top_k=5 drain 0.01 to balance.

Let's draft final code in my head and check for syntax.

```
def norm_project(a, target=1.0):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        return a
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, None)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15:
        return np.full(a.shape, target / a.size, dtype=np.float64)
    return a * (target / s)
```

`fast_conv`:
```
def fast_conv(a):
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
       return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    if n == 1:
       return np.array([a[0]*a[0]])
    size = 1 << (2*n - 2).bit_length()
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, size)
    out = np.fft.irfft(A*A, size)
    return out[:2*n-1]
```
Need `2*n-2` could be 0 for n=1; handled.

`conv_deriv`:
```
def conv_deriv(a, w):
    n=a.size
    if n==1:
       # conv RA len1, w len1? output [a0*w0]; slice [0:1]
       return w[:1].copy()
    size = 1 << (3*n - 3).bit_length()
    ra = np.fft.rfft(a[::-1], size)
    rw = np.fft.rfft(w, size)
    out = np.fft.irfft(ra*rw, size)
    return out[n-1:2*n-1]
```
For n=1, 3n-3=0, size1; out[0:1] works actually. Could remove special.

For a nonzero but convolution zeros (only one nonzero? shape? conv may zeros if a one nonzero? L= a0^2? score fine. If L near0? no.

`make_weights`:
```
def make_weights(a,gamma, mix=0.05, top_k=None):
   a=norm_project(a)
   c=fast_conv(a)
   L=c.size
   if gamma is None or gamma <=0:
      w=np.ones(L)/L
   else:
      cm=c.max()
      z=gamma*(c-cm)
      z-=z.max()
      w=np.exp(z)
      w=np.where(np.isfinite(w), w, 0.0)
      ws=w.sum()
      if ws <=0:
          idx=int(np.argmax(c)); w=np.zeros(L); w[idx]=1.0
      else: w/=ws
      if mix>0:
          w = (1-mix)*w + mix*(1.0/L)
   if top_k is not None:
      # blend top? maybe not
   return w
```
Maybe top_k weight hard not used.

Let's consider using a second-order exact method: Since objective is min t s.t. max c_k/S^2 ≤ t/(2n). For normalized sum1, constraints c_k ≤ C. At local optimum, active set A with c_k=C. We can solve a system where for active convex combination weights λ, gradient λ^T G_A = 0 (constant), and change a in tangent to maintain sum and active constraints? First-order conditions: For active constraints h(a)=c_k-C. If interior a, Lagrangian L=C? minimize C with a in simplex. If we treat C variable, F = max c. At optimum, ∇(λ^T c) + ν1 =0 for weights λ≥0, sumλ=1, λ_k(c_k-C)=0. We can solve for λ from KKT? Given active set, require ∇c active^T λ = constant vector. This is linear equations n constraints, λ size |A|. Also c active =C. We can adjust a to satisfy c active=C? But C unknown. This is nonlinear. Implement top-level `compute_dual_weights(a, top_k, include_drain)`:
```
def compute_dual_weights(a, k):
   a=normalize(a)
   c=fast_conv(a); cm=c.max()
   thr=cm - 0.05*cm
   idx=np.where(c>=thr)[0]
   if idx.size>k: idx=np.argpartition(c, -k)[-k:]
   idx=idx.astype(int)
   K=idx.size
   if K==0: idx=[argmax]; K=1
   # Build G rows: for each idx, G=2*conv_deriv(a, delta)
   M=[]
   for k in idx:
       w=np.zeros(c.size); w[k]=1.0
       M.append(2.0*conv_deriv(a,w))
   M=np.vstack(M)
   # A2 = [M, -1]
   A=np.empty((M.shape[0]+1, K+1))
   A[:-1,:K]=M; A[:-1,K:]=-1.0
   A[-1,:K]=1.0; A[-1,K:]=0.0
   b=np.zeros(M.shape[0]+1); b[-1]=1.0
   z,res,rank,sv=np.linalg.lstsq(A,b,rcond=None)
   lam=z[:K]
   lam=np.where(lam>0,lam,0)
   if lam.sum()<1e-12: lam=np.ones(K)/K
   lam/=lam.sum()
   return lam, idx
```
Cost: For K=20, each conv_deriv FFT length 32768; 20* maybe 0.02s? Good. Could be done occasionally.

Then use weights `w[idx]=lam` (zeros elsewhere) for weighted objective. But concentrated on top peaks; if λ has near zero, okay. This is exact active set. It might be better than smooth weights. We can blend with uniform for stability: `w_full[idx]=lam*0.95 + uniform*0.05`.

How to get G rows efficiently? Instead of allocating `w` and full conv each time, we can use formula: derivative for peak k: h_i = a_{k-i}+a_{i-k} valid. For each k, we can construct vector of length n directly O(K*n) without FFT, with n=7000, K=20 =>140k ops, cheap! Let's do that. Need derivative of c_k wrt a_i (not doubled by score). For peak k (0..2n-2): i such that k-i in [0,n-1]. For each i in intersection, derivative contribution 2*a_{k-i} except if i? Formula 2* for all. We can build row:
```
row=np.zeros(n)
j_start=max(0,k-n+1); j_end=min(k,n-1)
if j_end>=j_start:
   row[j_start:j_end+1] = 2.0 * a[k - j_end:k - j_start+1][::-1]
```
Check: For i=j, partner = k-i. So row[i]=2*a[k-i]. If i=k-i, row=2*a_i, correct. This is derivative of c_k. Good. No FFT needed! We can build M rows in O(K*n). Great.

Let's implement `build_peak_derivative(a,k)` top-level:
```
def peak_grad_one(a,k):
   n=a.size
   row=np.zeros(n)
   j0=max(0,k-n+1); j1=min(k,n-1)
   if j1>=j0:
       row[j0:j1+1] = 2.0 * a[k-j1:k-j0+1][::-1]
   return row
```

```
def peak_subgradient_refine(a0,max_iters,deadline,drain=0.02,k_max=12,step_init=0.03):
   a=norm_project(a0); best=a.copy(); best_sc=compute_score(a)
   v=zeros; step=step_init; use_dual_counter=0
   for it:
      if time>deadline break
      c=fast_conv(a); sc=2n*c.max(); update
      h, idx = dual_h_from_c(a,c,k_max,drain)
      g = h - h.mean()  # tangent to sum
      v = 0.8*v + g
      # scale v to norm 1
      vnorm=norm(v); if small break; v/=vnorm
      a_try=norm_project(a - step*v)
      sc_try=compute_score(a_try)
      if sc_try < best_sc:
         best=a_try; best_sc=sc_try; step=min(step*1.05,0.2); v maybe keep
      else:
         step *=0.8
   return best
```

def generate_candidates(n, rng, parent, base):
  cands=[]
  if base is not None: cands.append(base.copy())
  if parent is not None:
     xold=... if parent size !=n: p=np.interp(...)
     else p=parent
     for eps in [0.0,0.005,0.02,0.05]:
        q=p + eps*rng.standard_normal(n)
        q=np.maximum(q,0.0)
        if q.sum()>0: cands.append(q)
  x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
  cands.append(np.ones(n))
  # frequency candidates
  for freq in [1,2,3,5,8]:
     for amp in [0.1,0.3,0.5,0.7,0.9]:
        for ph in [0, np.pi/2, np.pi]:
           f=1+amp*np.cos(2*np.pi*freq*x+ph)
           f=np.maximum(f,1e-6)
           if f.sum()>0: cands.append(f)
  # skew polynomials
  for p in [0.5,1,2,4]:
     cands.append(x**p)
     cands.append((1-x)**p)
     cands.append(x**p*(1-x)**0.5+1e-6)
     cands.append((1-x)**p*x**0.5+1e-6)
  # random Fourier
  for _ in range(12):
     sig=0
     for m in [1,2,3,5,7,11,16]:
        amp=rng.uniform(0,0.4)/(1+0.3*m)
        ph=rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)
        sig += amp*np.cos(2*np.pi*m*x+ph)
     f=1+0.6*sig
     f=np.maximum(f,1e-6)
     cands.append(f)
  return cands
```

```python
"""
Hybrid search for low peak self-convolution coefficients.

Main ingredients:
- FFT-based exact score and smooth LSE score/gradient.
- L-BFGS in three forms:
  a) raw nonnegative coefficients,
  b) log/softmax reparameterization,
  c) actively weighted peak objective from a primal-dual active-set solve.
- Exact active-set weight computation using the gradients of the current
  top convolution coefficients.
- Projected subgradient polishing using those active-set weights.
"""

import time
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize


class TimeLimitException(Exception):
    pass


_DEADLINE = float("inf")
_LAST_RAW_X = None
_LAST_V_X = None
_LAST_W_X = None


def norm_project(a, target=1.0):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        return a
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, None)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15:
        return np.full(a.shape, float(target) / float(a.size), dtype=np.float64)
    return a * (float(target) / s)


def fast_conv(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    if n == 1:
        return np.array([a[0] * a[0]], dtype=np.float64)
    size = 1 << (2 * n - 2).bit_length()
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, size)
    out = np.fft.irfft(A * A, size)
    return out[: 2 * n - 1].astype(np.float64, copy=False)


def conv_deriv(a, w):
    """Derivative helper: for h_i = sum_k w_k a_{k-i}, return h."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    size = 1 << (3 * n - 3).bit_length()
    ra = np.fft.rfft(a[::-1], size)
    rw = np.fft.rfft(w, size)
    out = np.fft.irfft(ra * rw, size)
    return out[n - 1 : 2 * n - 1].astype(np.float64, copy=False)


def compute_score(a):
    a = norm_project(a)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")
    c = fast_conv(a)
    return float(2.0 * n * float(np.max(c)))


def lse_score_grad(a, beta):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-12:
        return 1e100, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    c = fast_conv(a)
    cm = float(np.max(c))
    if beta <= 0.0:
        beta = 1.0

    z = beta * (c - cm)
    z = z - float(np.max(z))
    e = np.exp(z)
    es = float(e.sum())
    if es <= 0.0:
        p = np.zeros(c.size, dtype=np.float64)
        p[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
    else:
        p = e / es

    L = cm + float(np.log(es)) / beta
    score = (2.0 * n * L) / (s * s)

    h = 2.0 * conv_deriv(a, p)
    ga = (2.0 * n) * (h * s - 2.0 * L) / (s ** 3)

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(ga)):
        ga = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    return float(score), ga.astype(np.float64, copy=False)


def softmax_from_v(v):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
    v = v - float(np.mean(v))
    v = np.clip(v, -50.0, 50.0)
    e = np.exp(v)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        return np.full(v.shape, 1.0 / v.size, dtype=np.float64)
    return e / s


def softmax_score_jac_v(x, beta):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    a = softmax_from_v(x)
    sc, ga = lse_score_grad(a, beta)
    dot = float(np.dot(a, ga))
    gv = a * ga - a * dot
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(gv)):
        gv = np.zeros_like(a)
    return float(sc), gv.astype(np.float64, copy=False)


def weighted_score_grad(a, w):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-12:
        return 1e100, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    c = fast_conv(a)
    wn = float(np.dot(w, c))
    score = (2.0 * n * wn) / (s * s)
    h = 2.0 * conv_deriv(a, w)
    ga = (2.0 * n) * (h * s - 2.0 * wn) / (s ** 3)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(ga)):
        ga = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    return float(score), ga.astype(np.float64, copy=False)


def time_callback(x):
    if time.monotonic() > _DEADLINE:
        raise TimeLimitException


def raw_obj(x, beta):
    global _LAST_RAW_X
    if time.monotonic() > _DEADLINE:
        raise TimeLimitException
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    _LAST_RAW_X = x.copy()
    sc, ga = lse_score_grad(x, beta)
    # Very light scale anchor to avoid drift in the log-sum-exp surrogate.
    s = float(x.sum())
    pen = 1e-4
    sc += pen * (s - 1.0) * (s - 1.0)
    if s > 0.0:
        ga = ga + (2.0 * pen * (s - 1.0)) * np.ones_like(x, dtype=np.float64)
    return float(sc), ga


def v_obj(x, beta):
    global _LAST_V_X
    if time.monotonic() > _DEADLINE:
        raise TimeLimitException
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    _LAST_V_X = x.copy()
    return softmax_score_jac_v(x, beta)


def w_obj(x, w):
    global _LAST_W_X
    if time.monotonic() > _DEADLINE:
        raise TimeLimitException
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    _LAST_W_X = x.copy()
    return weighted_score_grad(x, w)


def _lbfgs_options(maxiter, maxfun, gtol):
    return {
        "maxiter": int(maxiter),
        "ftol": 1e-15,
        "gtol": float(gtol),
        "maxfun": int(maxfun),
        "maxls": 25,
        "m": 30,
    }


def run_raw_lbfgs(a0, beta, deadline, maxiter=1500, maxfun=30000, gtol=1e-11):
    global _DEADLINE, _LAST_RAW_X
    a0 = norm_project(a0)
    n = a0.size
    _DEADLINE = float(deadline)
    _LAST_RAW_X = None

    bounds = [(0.0, 1000.0)] * n
    opts = _lbfgs_options(maxiter, maxfun, gtol)

    try:
        res = minimize(
            raw_obj,
            a0,
            args=(beta,),
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            jac=True,
            bounds=bounds,
            callback=time_callback,
            options=opts,
        )
        a = res.x
    except TimeLimitException:
        a = _LAST_RAW_X if _LAST_RAW_X is not None else a0
    except Exception:
        a = a0

    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size != n:
        a = a0
    return norm_project(a)


def run_v_lbfgs(a0, beta, deadline, maxiter=1200, maxfun=30000, gtol=1e-11):
    global _DEADLINE, _LAST_V_X
    a0 = norm_project(a0)
    n = a0.size
    v0 = np.log(np.maximum(a0, 1e-12))
    v0 = v0 - float(np.mean(v0))
    _DEADLINE = float(deadline)
    _LAST_V_X = None

    opts = _lbfgs_options(maxiter, maxfun, gtol)

    try:
        res = minimize(
            v_obj,
            v0,
            args=(beta,),
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            jac=True,
            bounds=None,
            callback=time_callback,
            options=opts,
        )
        v = res.x
    except TimeLimitException:
        v = _LAST_V_X if _LAST_V_X is not None else v0
    except Exception:
        v = v0

    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
    if v.size != n:
        v = v0
    a = softmax_from_v(v)
    return norm_project(a)


def run_weighted_lbfgs(a0, w, deadline, maxiter=1500, maxfun=30000, gtol=1e-11):
    global _DEADLINE, _LAST_W_X
    a0 = norm_project(a0)
    n = a0.size
    w = norm_project(w, target=1.0)
    if w.size != 2 * n - 1:
        w = np.ones(2 * n - 1, dtype=np.float64) / (2 * n - 1)

    _DEADLINE = float(deadline)
    _LAST_W_X = None

    bounds = [(0.0, 1000.0)] * n
    opts = _lbfgs_options(maxiter, maxfun, gtol)

    try:
        res = minimize(
            w_obj,
            a0,
            args=(w,),
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            jac=True,
            bounds=bounds,
            callback=time_callback,
            options=opts,
        )
        a = res.x
    except TimeLimitException:
        a = _LAST_W_X if _LAST_W_X is not None else a0
    except Exception:
        a = a0

    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size != n:
        a = a0
    return norm_project(a)


def peak_grad_one(a, k):
    """Gradient of one convolution coefficient c_k with respect to a."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    row = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    j0 = max(0, k - n + 1)
    j1 = min(k, n - 1)
    if j1 >= j0:
        row[j0 : j1 + 1] = 2.0 * a[k - j1 : k - j0 + 1][::-1]
    return row


def dual_h_from_c(a, c, k_max=24, drain=0.02):
    """
    Given c = a*a convolution, choose a nonnegative convex combination of
    gradients of near-max coefficients so that the combined gradient is as
    constant as possible.  This provides active-set dual weights.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    k_max = int(max(1, k_max))

    cm = float(np.max(c))
    thr = cm - max(0.0, drain) * max(1e-300, cm)
    idx = np.where(c >= thr)[0]
    if idx.size == 0:
        idx = np.array([int(np.argmax(c))], dtype=int)
    if idx.size > k_max:
        idx = np.argpartition(c, -k_max)[-k_max:].astype(int)
    idx = np.asarray(idx, dtype=int)
    K = idx.size

    rows = []
    for kk in idx:
        rows.append(peak_grad_one(a, int(kk)))
    M = np.vstack(rows).astype(np.float64, copy=False)

    if K == 1:
        lam = np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
        h = M[0]
        return h, idx

    # Solve least-squares: M lam - alpha * 1 = 0, sum(lam)=1.
    A = np.empty((M.shape[0] + 1, K + 1), dtype=np.float64)
    A[:-1, :K] = M
    A[:-1, K:] = -1.0
    A[-1, :K] = 1.0
    A[-1, K:] = 0.0
    b = np.zeros(M.shape[0] + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    b[-1] = 1.0

    lam = None
    try:
        z, *_ = np.linalg.lstsq(A, b, rcond=None)
        lam = np.asarray(z[:K], dtype=np.float64)
    except Exception:
        lam = None

    if lam is None or not np.all(np.isfinite(lam)):
        lam = np.ones(K, dtype=np.float64) / K
    else:
        lam = np.where(lam > 0.0, lam, 0.0)
        ss = float(lam.sum())
        if ss <= 1e-12:
            lam = np.ones(K, dtype=np.float64) / K
        else:
            lam = lam / ss

    h = M.dot(lam)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(h)):
        h = np.mean(M, axis=0)
    return h.astype(np.float64, copy=False), idx


def weights_from_dual(a, k_max=24, drain=0.02, mix=0.05):
    a = norm_project(a)
    c = fast_conv(a)
    h, idx = dual_h_from_c(a, c, k_max=k_max, drain=drain)
    n = a.size
    w = np.zeros(2 * n - 1, dtype=np.float64)
    w[idx] = 1.0 / max(1, idx.size)

    # For the weighted L-BFGS, use a geometric mix with a flat component.
    if mix > 0.0:
        w = (1.0 - mix) * w + mix * (1.0 / w.size)
    return norm_project(w, target=1.0)


def peak_subgradient_refine(a0, max_iters=2000, deadline=float("inf"),
                            drain=0.01, k_max=12, step_init=0.005):
    a = norm_project(a0)
    n = a.size
    best = a.copy()
    best_sc = compute_score(a)

    vel = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    step = float(step_init)
    last_refresh = -10

    for it in range(int(max_iters)):
        if time.monotonic() > float(deadline):
            break

        c = fast_conv(a)
        sc = float(2.0 * n * np.max(c))
        if sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best = a.copy()

        if it - last_refresh >= 3 or not np.any(a):
            h, _ = dual_h_from_c(a, c, k_max=k_max, drain=drain)
            last_refresh = it
        else:
            # A cheap fallback if cache gets stale: equal top average is not maintained,
            # so just reuse the last h. If it is zero, recompute.
            if not np.any(h):
                h, _ = dual_h_from_c(a, c, k_max=k_max, drain=drain)
                last_refresh = it

        g = h - float(np.mean(h))
        vel = 0.75 * vel + g
        gn = float(np.linalg.norm(vel))
        if gn < 1e-16:
            break
        d = vel / gn

        a_try = norm_project(a - step * d)
        sc_try = compute_score(a_try)
        if sc_try < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc_try
            best = a_try.copy()
            step = min(step * 1.05, 0.02)
        else:
            step *= 0.78
            if step < 1e-6:
                step = 1e-6

    return best


def generate_candidates(n, rng, parent, base):
    cands = []

    if base is not None:
        cands.append(np.asarray(base, dtype=np.float64).copy())

    if parent is not None:
        p = np.asarray(parent, dtype=np.float64)
        if p.size != n:
            xold = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, p.size)
            xnew = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
            p = np.interp(xnew, xold, p)
        for eps in (0.0, 0.005, 0.02, 0.06):
            q = p.copy()
            if eps > 0.0:
                q = q + eps * rng.standard_normal(n)
            q = np.maximum(q, 0.0)
            if q.sum() > 0.0:
                cands.append(q)

    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    cands.append(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))

    for freq in (1, 2, 3, 5, 8):
        for amp in (0.1, 0.3, 0.5, 0.7, 0.9):
            for ph in (0.0, 0.5 * np.pi, np.pi):
                f = 1.0 + amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * freq * x + ph)
                f = np.maximum(f, 1e-6)
                cands.append(f.astype(np.float64))

    for p1 in (0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 4.0):
        cands.append((x ** p1).astype(np.float64))
        cands.append(((1.0 - x) ** p1).astype(np.float64))
        cands.append((x ** p1 * (1.0 - x) ** 0.5 + 1e-6).astype(np.float64))
        cands.append(((1.0 - x) ** p1 * x ** 0.5 + 1e-6).astype(np.float64))

    for _ in range(12):
        sig = 0.0
        for m in (1, 2, 3, 5, 7, 11, 16):
            amp = rng.uniform(0.0, 0.4) / (1.0 + 0.3 * m)
            ph = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)
            sig += amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * m * x + ph)
        f = 1.0 + 0.6 * sig
        f = np.maximum(f, 1e-6)
        cands.append(f.astype(np.float64))

    return cands


def safe_evaluate(seq):
    try:
        out = []
        for x in seq:
            y = float(x)
            if not np.isfinite(y):
                y = 0.0
            if y < 0.0:
                y = 0.0
            elif y > 1000.0:
                y = 1000.0
            out.append(y)
        val = evaluate_sequence(out)
        return float(val)
    except Exception:
        return float("inf")


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400):
    global _DEADLINE, _LAST_RAW_X, _LAST_V_X, _LAST_W_X

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    total = float(budget_s)
    final_reserve = 70.0
    final_deadline = t0 + max(30.0, total - final_reserve)
    _DEADLINE = final_deadline

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    # ---------------- Load parent if available ----------------
    parent = None
    try:
        h = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
        if h is not None:
            arr = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
            arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
            if arr.size >= 100:
                parent = arr
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    if parent is not None and 2000 <= parent.size <= 10000:
        n = int(parent.size)
    else:
        n = 6962

    if parent is None:
        a0 = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) * 0.5
    else:
        if parent.size == n:
            a0 = parent.astype(np.float64, copy=True)
        else:
            xold = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, parent.size)
            xnew = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
            a0 = np.interp(xnew, xold, parent)

    best_a = norm_project(a0)
    best_sc = compute_score(best_a)
    print(f"Initial n={n}, internal score={best_sc:.9f}")

    # ---------------- Candidate scan ----------------
    cands = generate_candidates(n, rng, parent, best_a)
    scored = []
    for i, cd in enumerate(cands):
        cd = norm_project(cd)
        sc = compute_score(cd)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            scored.append((sc, i, cd))
    scored.sort()

    for sc, _, cd in scored:
        if sc < best_sc - 1e-14:
            best_a = cd.copy()
            best_sc = sc
            print(f"  Candidate scan improved to {best_sc:.9f}")

    # If the parent was not available, spend a bit more time polishing the
    # best diverse starts before the main regime.
    if parent is None or best_sc > 1.65:
        quick_deadline = t0 + min(300.0, 0.18 * max(10.0, total - 60.0))
        for rank in range(min(3, len(scored))):
            if time.monotonic() > quick_deadline:
                break
            sc0, _, a0 = scored[rank]
            if sc0 > best_sc + 1e-10:
                continue
            beta = float(n)
            end = min(time.monotonic() + 90.0, quick_deadline, final_deadline - 120.0)
            a2 = run_raw_lbfgs(a0, beta, end, maxiter=1200, maxfun=25000, gtol=1e-11)
            sc2 = compute_score(a2)
            if sc2 < best_sc - 1e-14:
                best_a = a2.copy()
                best_sc = sc2
                print(f"  Quick diverse refinement improved to {best_sc:.9f}")

    # If we already found something safely below target, verify and return early.
    if best_sc < 1.5000:
        out = [float(x) for x in norm_project(best_a)]
        ver = safe_evaluate(out)
        if np.isfinite(ver) and ver < 1.5027:
            print(f"Early verified win: {ver:.9f}")
            return out

    # ---------------- Main alternating refinement ----------------
    main_end = max(time.monotonic(), final_deadline - 260.0)
    beta_coeffs = (0.1, 1.0, 3.0, 10.0, 30.0, 100.0)
    cycle = 0
    last_improve_time = time.monotonic()

    while time.monotonic() < main_end:
        start_a = best_a.copy()

        if time.monotonic() - last_improve_time > 720.0:
            noise = 0.25 / float(n)
            start_a = norm_project(best_a + rng.standard_normal(n) * noise)

        beta = float(n) * float(beta_coeffs[cycle % len(beta_coeffs)])
        chunk_end = min(time.monotonic() + 45.0, main_end)

        if cycle % 6 == 0:
            a2 = run_raw_lbfgs(start_a, beta, chunk_end,
                               maxiter=1800, maxfun=40000, gtol=1e-11)
        elif cycle % 6 == 1:
            w = weights_from_dual(start_a, k_max=24, drain=0.02, mix=0.05)
            a2 = run_weighted_lbfgs(start_a, w, chunk_end,
                                    maxiter=1800, maxfun=40000, gtol=1e-11)
        elif cycle % 6 == 2:
            a2 = run_v_lbfgs(start_a, beta, chunk_end,
                             maxiter=1200, maxfun=30000, gtol=1e-11)
        elif cycle % 6 == 3:
            a2 = run_raw_lbfgs(start_a, beta * 3.0, chunk_end,
                               maxiter=1800, maxfun=40000, gtol=1e-11)
        elif cycle % 6 == 4:
            a2 = peak_subgradient_refine(start_a, max_iters=2000, deadline=chunk_end,
                                         drain=0.005, k_max=10, step_init=0.004)
        else:
            w = weights_from_dual(start_a, k_max=36, drain=0.01, mix=0.02)
            a2 = run_weighted_lbfgs(start_a, w, chunk_end,
                                    maxiter=1800, maxfun=40000, gtol=1e-11)

        sc2 = compute_score(a2)
        if sc2 < best_sc - 1e-14:
            best_a = a2.copy()
            best_sc = sc2
            last_improve_time = time.monotonic()
            print(f"  cycle {cycle}: score={best_sc:.9f}")

        cycle += 1

    # ---------------- Final dual/subgradient polish ----------------
    t = time.monotonic()
    if t < final_deadline - 90.0:
        end = min(t + 45.0, final_deadline - 90.0)
        a2 = peak_subgradient_refine(best_a, max_iters=2500, deadline=end,
                                     drain=0.0, k_max=8, step_init=0.002)
        sc2 = compute_score(a2)
        if sc2 < best_sc - 1e-14:
            best_a = a2.copy()
            best_sc = sc2
            print(f"  final dual subgradient: {best_sc:.9f}")

    t = time.monotonic()
    if t < final_deadline - 50.0:
        end = min(t + 30.0, final_deadline - 50.0)
        a2 = peak_subgradient_refine(best_a, max_iters=2500, deadline=end,
                                     drain=0.02, k_max=14, step_init=0.002)
        sc2 = compute_score(a2)
        if sc2 < best_sc - 1e-14:
            best_a = a2.copy()
            best_sc = sc2
            print(f"  final broad dual subgradient: {best_sc:.9f}")

    # ---------------- Very final high-beta raw LSE ----------------
    t = time.monotonic()
    if t < final_deadline - 30.0:
        end = min(t + 20.0, final_deadline - 25.0)
        beta = 50.0 * float(n)
        a2 = run_raw_lbfgs(best_a, beta, end, maxiter=2000, maxfun=50000, gtol=1e-12)
        sc2 = compute_score(a2)
        if sc2 < best_sc - 1e-14:
            best_a = a2.copy()
            best_sc = sc2
            print(f"  final raw LSE: {best_sc:.9f}")

    out_arr = norm_project(best_a)
    out = [float(x) for x in out_arr]

    verified = safe_evaluate(out)
    elapsed = time.monotonic() - t0
    print("=" * 70)
    print(f"FINAL internal score = {best_sc:.9f}")
    print(f"FINAL verified score = {verified:.9f}")
    print(f"Length = {len(out)}, time = {elapsed:.1f}s / {total:.1f}s")
    print("=" * 70)

    if not np.isfinite(verified):
        # Extremely defensive fallback.
        out = [0.5 / n] + [0.0] * (n - 1) + [0.5 / n]

    return out
```
